| 2026-09-02 | kyaw-2023-effect-chemical-electrochemical-decontamination-protocols | evidence-level-judgment | evidence_level: rct -> in-vitro. Abstract methods describe 90 bench-assembled implant+HA specimens (80 patient-retrieved, contaminated ex vivo) allocated to a decontamination-protocol group, then assessed entirely by bench methods (micro-CT, methylene-blue dye penetration, SEM/EDX) -- no living patient received a randomized clinical intervention or had a clinical outcome measured. "Randomized" describes specimen allocation to cleaning protocol, not patient randomization. Matches in-vitro (bench/laboratory study) per INGEST.md vocabulary, on par with the other in-vitro papers in the same HA-reuse batch (burioni-2024, cetinsoy-2026, naghsh-2024, abreu-2024), not above them. Flagged during the 2026-09-02 healing-abutment-reuse-single-use-controversy-overview review; corrected on both tiers plus 6 sibling Related-Papers mentions and the overview page (prose + Evidence Map table). |

| 2026-10-04 | troiano-2018-early-late-failure-submerged | relation-judgment | Scientific tension surfaced honestly rather than reconciled. 3 typed edges, each chosen from the content relationship: (1) extends -> early-implant-failure-risk-prevention-overview (that overview explicitly names this paper as the baseline SR it could not use, and its healing-protocol section previously rested only on confounded retrospective cohorts; this adds the missing pooled SR+MA/TSA stratum, and its caution against individualising by protocol for smokers is NOT overturned since no smoking stratification is reported here); (2) refines NOT reinforces -> giok-2026-factors-implant-failure-umbrella-review (Giok grades the submerged-vs-nonsubmerged contrast as high-certainty from RCTs but, being abstract-only, is direction-less; Troiano names the penalized arm and adds the TSA power verdict). Deliberately avoided 'reinforces' on Giok because Troiano 2018 is plausibly one of Giok's 25 constituent meta-analyses, which would make a reinforcing edge near-circular in substance; that constituent hypothesis is flagged as an inference in the page body. (3) reinforces -> guarnieri-2025-analysis-risk-factors-related-early (independent 930-implant GEE cohort, different design/team/era, same non-submerged direction at pooled level). WITHHELD 'contradicts' against Moustafa Ali 2018 (Int J Periodontics Restor Dent, 10.11607/ijp.5315) — the second 2018 SR+MA on the identical submerged-vs-non-submerged comparison reporting NO failure difference and MORE marginal bone loss with submerged placement. Two SR+MAs of equal weight genuinely conflict on the early-failure outcome, but no such stem exists in this wiki (qmd search 'Moustafa Ali' and '10.11607/ijp.5315' both returned no results; relations-audit.py requires target to be an existing stem), so the conflict is recorded in prose in both Related Papers sections instead and the pair is flagged as not-yet-ingested. Note the two papers CONVERGE on bone level (both favour non-submerged preservation) and DIVERGE only on early failure - stated that way rather than flattened into 'they disagree'. No supersession: the two older retrospective pages keep standing as their own evidence stratum and nothing held is overturned. |
